([muratmirgun/gophers](https://github.com/muratmirgun/gophers/blob/HEAD/agents/README.md) · ★9)

- **README**: Every agent lives in a collective directory that includes an `AGENTS.md` marker and an `agents/` folder. The **agent identifier** is derived from its file path inside that folder, e.g. `.genie/code/agents/review.md` → `code/review`. If an agent sits at the workspace root (`.genie/agents/review.md`) it keeps the simple id `review`. Rename or relocate the markdown file to change the id—no extra meta ([namastexlabs/automagik-cli](https://github.com/namastexlabs/automagik-cli/blob/HEAD/.genie/agents/README.md) · ★6)
- **README**: &gt; 33 autonomous AI specialists. Each one encodes deep domain expertise — security patterns, architecture principles, testing strategies, growth playbooks — so you get senior-level output without senior-level effort. ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/README.md) · ★6)
- **README**: This plugin's wiki capabilities are implemented as **skills**, not sub-agents. They run inline in the calling session rather than as isolated sub-agents. ([richfrem/agent-plugins-skills](https://github.com/richfrem/agent-plugins-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/obsidian-wiki-engine/agents/README.md) · ★6)
- **README**: The plugin's shipped subagents — the roles bro dispatches a code-touching task to. Each file is a Claude Code agent definition: YAML frontmatter (name, model, tool allow-list, attached skills) followed by the persona prompt. bro itself is defined by the plugin-root `CLAUDE.md`, so it lives outside this directory; only the agents bro spawns are here. ([trustmybot/plugin](https://github.com/trustmybot/plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/README.md) · ★6)
- **README**: `knowledge.edn` is the single structured source for Wagoe's agent guardrails.
